1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the rings structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 288. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 973 and converges at rate O(1/n^1).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 288 + O(1/n^973). Summing over all modes and applying the rings convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

This completes the proof. QED.
